- **aviso-revision**: Skill FULL del módulo PUENTE (stateless) `aviso-revision` de la vertical contabilidad (Enki). El EMPUJÓN al canal de avisos: 'esto necesita revisión'. - **avoid-any**: Handle `any` correctly — it is not a type but a directive that disables type checking. Substitute by position (assignee → `unknown`, assigned → `never`). Also covers the `any` that is never written down: a precise signature fed `any` at every call site, which no lint rule and no compiler check can report. ([jiayaoqijia/cryptoskill](https://github.com/jiayaoqijia/cryptoskill/tree/HEAD/skills/wallets/metamask-official-avoid-any) · ★81)
